= SNAIL - SOTAI
:toc:

== SNAIL

* `Lev 11:30` The ferret, the chameleon, the lizard, the *_snail_*, and the mole.
* `Psa 58:8` Let them be like a *_snail_* which melts and passes away, like the untimely birth of a woman that has not seen the sun.

* `Psa 107:4` They wandered in the wilderness in a *_solitary_* way. They found no city to dwell in.
* `Isa 35:1` The wilderness and the *_solitary_* place shall be glad for them, and the desert shall rejoice and blossom like the rose.
* `Lam 1:1` [ALEPH] How the city sits *_solitary_* that was full of people! How she has become like a widow! She who was great among the nations and princess among the provinces, how she has become tributary!
* `Mrk 1:35` And in the morning, rising a great while before day, he went out and departed into a *_solitary_* place and prayed there.

* `1Ch 2:52` And Shobal the father of Kirjath-jearim had *_sons_*: Haroeh and half of the Manahethites.
* `1Ch 2:54` The *_sons_* of Salma: Beth-lehem and the Netophathites, Ataroth-beth-joab, half of the Menahethites, and the Zorites.
* `1Ch 3:1` Now these were the *_sons_* of David who were born to him in Hebron: the first-born Amnon, of Ahinoam the Jezreelitess; the second Daniel, of Abigail the Carmelitess;
* `1Ch 3:9` These were all the *_sons_* of David, besides the *_sons_* of the concubines and Tamar their sister.
* `1Ch 3:15` And the *_sons_* of Josiah were the first-born Johanan, the second Jehoiakim, the third Zedekiah, the fourth Shallum.
* `1Ch 3:16` And the *_sons_* of Jehoiakim: Jeconiah his son and Zedekiah his son.
* `1Ch 3:17` And the *_sons_* of Jeconiah: Assir, Salathiel his son,
* `1Ch 3:19` And the *_sons_* of Pedaiah were Zerubbabel and Shimei. And the *_sons_* of Zerubbabel: Meshullam and Hananiah, and Shelomith was their sister;
* `1Ch 3:21` And the *_sons_* of Hananiah: Pelatiah, Jesaiah, the *_sons_* of Rephaiah, the *_sons_* of Arnan, the *_sons_* of Obadiah, the *_sons_* of Shechaniah.
* `1Ch 3:22` And the son of Shechaniah: Shemaiah. And the *_sons_* of Shemaiah: Hattush, Igeal, Bariah, Neariah, and Shaphat, six.
* `1Ch 3:23` And the *_sons_* of Neariah: Elioenai, Hezekiah, and Azrikam, three.
* `1Ch 3:24` And the *_sons_* of Elioenai were Hodaiah, Eliashib, Pelaiah, Akkub, Johanan, Delaiah, and Anani, seven.
* `1Ch 4:1` The *_sons_* of Judah: Pharez, Hezron, Carmi, Hur, and Shobal.
* `1Ch 4:3` And these were the *_sons_* of the father of Etam: Jezreel, Ishma, and Idbash, and the name of their sister was Hazelelponi,
* `1Ch 4:4` And Penuel the father of Gedor, and Ezer the father of Hushah. These are the *_sons_* of Hur, the first-born of Ephratah, the father of Beth-lehem.
* `1Ch 4:6` And Naarah bore him Ahuzam, Hepher, Temeni, and Haahashtari. These were the *_sons_* of Naarah.
* `1Ch 4:7` And the *_sons_* of Helah were Zereth, Jezoar, and Ethnan.
* `1Ch 4:13` And the *_sons_* of Kenaz: Othniel and Seraiah. And the *_sons_* of Othniel: Hathath,
* `1Ch 4:15` And the *_sons_* of Caleb the son of Jephunneh: Iru, Elah, and Naam. And the son of Elah: Kenaz.
* `1Ch 4:16` And the *_sons_* of Jehaleleel: Ziph, Ziphah, Tiria, and Asareel.
* `1Ch 4:17` And the *_sons_* of Ezra were Jethur, Mered, Epher, and Jalon. Mered took a wife and she bore Miriam, Shammai, and Ishbah the father of Eshtemoa.
* `1Ch 4:18` And his wife Jehudijah bore Jered the father of Gedor, Heber the father of Socho, and Jekuthiel the father of Zanoah. And these are the *_sons_* of Bithiah the daughter of Pharaoh whom Mered took.
* `1Ch 4:19` And the *_sons_* of his wife Hodiah the sister of Naham: the father of Keilah the Garmite, and Eshtemoa the Maachathite.
* `1Ch 4:20` And the *_sons_* of Shimon were Amnon, Rinnah, Ben-hanan, and Tilon. And the *_sons_* of Ishi were Zoheth and Benzoheth.
* `1Ch 4:21` The *_sons_* of Shelah the son of Judah were Er the father of Lecah, Laadah the father of Mareshah, the families of the house of those who wrought fine linen, of the house of Ashbea,
* `1Ch 4:24` The *_sons_* of Simeon were Nemuel, Jamin, Jarib, Zerah, and Shaul;
* `1Ch 4:26` And the *_sons_* of Mishma: Hamuel his son, Zacchur his son, and Shimei his son.
* `1Ch 4:27` And Shimei had sixteen *_sons_* and six daughters, but his brothers did not have many children, neither did all their family multiply like the children of Judah.
* `1Ch 4:42` And some of them from the *_sons_* of Simeon, five hundred men, went to mount Seir, having for their captains Pelatiah, Neariah, Rephaiah, and Uzziel, the *_sons_* of Ishi.
* `1Ch 5:1` Now the *_sons_* of Reuben the first-born of Israel (for he was the first-born, but because he defiled his father's bed, his birth-right was given to the *_sons_* of Joseph the son of Israel and the genealogy is not to be reckoned after the birth-right;
* `1Ch 5:3` The *_sons_* of Reuben the first-born of Israel were Hanoch, Pallu, Hezron, and Carmi.
* `1Ch 5:4` The *_sons_* of Joel: Shemaiah his son, Gog his son, Shimei his son,
* `1Ch 5:18` The *_sons_* of Reuben, the Gadites, and half the tribe of Manasseh, all who were valiant men—men able to bear buckler and sword, to shoot with bow, and skillful in war—forty-four thousand seven hundred and sixty who were able to go forth to war.
* `1Ch 6:1` The *_sons_* of Levi: Gershon, Kohath, and Merari.
* `1Ch 6:2` And the *_sons_* of Kohath: Amram, Izhar, Hebron, and Uzziel.
* `1Ch 6:3` And the children of Amram: Aaron, Moses, and Miriam. The *_sons_* also of Aaron: Nadab, Abihu, Eleazar, and Ithamar.
* `1Ch 6:16` The *_sons_* of Levi: Gershom, Kohath, and Merari.
* `1Ch 6:17` And these are the names of the *_sons_* of Gershom: Libni and Shimei.
* `1Ch 6:18` And the *_sons_* of Kohath were Amram, Izhar, Hebron, and Uzziel.
* `1Ch 6:19` The *_sons_* of Merari: Mahli and Mushi. And these are the families of the Levites according to their fathers.
* `1Ch 6:22` The *_sons_* of Kohath: Amminadab his son, Korah his son, Assir his son,
* `1Ch 6:25` And the *_sons_* of Elkanah: Amasai and Ahimoth.
* `1Ch 6:26` As for Elkanah, the *_sons_* of Elkanah: Zophai his son, Nahath his son,
* `1Ch 6:28` And the *_sons_* of Samuel: the first-born Vashni and Abiah.
* `1Ch 6:29` The *_sons_* of Merari: Mahli, Libni his son, Shimei his son, Uzza his son,
* `1Ch 6:33` And these are those who waited with their children. Of the *_sons_* of the Kohathites: Heman a singer, the son of Joel, the son of Shemuel,
* `1Ch 6:44` And their brothers the *_sons_* of Merari stood on the left hand: Ethan the son of Kishi, the son of Abdi, the son of Malluch,
* `1Ch 6:49` But Aaron and his *_sons_* offered upon the altar of the burnt-offering and on the altar of incense, and were appointed for all the work of the holy of holies and to make an atonement for Israel, according to all that Moses the servant of God had commanded.
* `1Ch 6:50` And these are the *_sons_* of Aaron: Eleazar his son, Phinehas his son, Abishua his son,
* `1Ch 6:54` Now these are their dwelling-places throughout their encampments in their limits: to the *_sons_* of Aaron from the families of the Kohathites, for theirs was the lot,
* `1Ch 6:57` And to the *_sons_* of Aaron they gave the cities of Judah, namely, Hebron (the city of refuge), Libnah with its suburbs, Jattir, Eshtemoa with its suburbs,
* `1Ch 6:61` And to the *_sons_* of Kohath who were left of the family of that tribe, cities were given by lot out of the half tribe, namely, out of the half tribe of Manasseh, ten cities.
* `1Ch 6:62` And to the *_sons_* of Gershom throughout their families were given out of the tribe of Issachar, out of the tribe of Asher, out of the tribe of Naphtali, and out of the tribe of Manasseh in Bashan, thirteen cities.
* `1Ch 6:63` To the *_sons_* of Merari were given by lot throughout their families, out of the tribe of Reuben, out of the tribe of Gad, and out of the tribe of Zebulun, twelve cities.
* `1Ch 6:66` And some of the families of the *_sons_* of Kohath had cities of their borders out of the tribe of Ephraim.
* `1Ch 6:70` And out of the half tribe of Manasseh: Aner with its suburbs and Bileam with its suburbs for the family of the remnant of the *_sons_* of Kohath.
* `1Ch 6:71` To the *_sons_* of Gershom were given out of the family of the half tribe of Manasseh, Golan in Bashan with its suburbs and Ashtaroth with its suburbs.
* `1Ch 7:1` Now the *_sons_* of Issachar were Tola, Puah, Jashub, and Shimron, four.
* `1Ch 7:2` And the *_sons_* of Tola: Uzzi, Rephaiah, Jeriel, Jahmai, Jibsam, and Shemuel, heads of their father's house, namely, of Tola. They were valiant men of might in their generations whose number was in the days of David twenty-two thousand and six hundred.
* `1Ch 7:3` And the son of Uzzi: Izrahiah. And the *_sons_* of Izrahiah: Michael, Obadiah, Joel, and Ishaiah, five. All of them were chief men.
* `1Ch 7:4` And with them, by their generations, after the house of their fathers, were bands of soldiers for war, thirty-six thousand men, for they had many wives and *_sons_*.
* `1Ch 7:6` The *_sons_* of Benjamin: Bela, Becher, and Jediael, three.
* `1Ch 7:7` And the *_sons_* of Bela: Ezbon, Uzzi, Uzziel, Jerimoth, and Iri, five, heads of the house of their fathers, mighty men of valor. They were reckoned by their genealogies twenty-two thousand and thirty-four.
* `1Ch 7:8` And the *_sons_* of Becher: Zemira, Joash, Eliezer, Elioenai, Omri, Jerimoth, Abiah, Anathoth, and Alameth. All these are the *_sons_* of Becher.
* `1Ch 7:10` The son of Jediael: Bilhan. And the *_sons_* of Bilhan: Jeush, Benjamin, Ehud, Chenaanah, Zethan, Tarshish, and Ahishahar.
* `1Ch 7:11` All these the *_sons_* of Jediael by the heads of their fathers, mighty men of valor, were seventeen thousand and two hundred soldiers fit to go out for war and battle.
* `1Ch 7:13` The *_sons_* of Naphtali: Jahziel, Guni, Jezer, and Shallum, the *_sons_* of Bilhah.
* `1Ch 7:14` The *_sons_* of Manasseh: Ashriel, whom his concubine the Aramitess bore (she bore Machir the father of Gilead,
* `1Ch 7:16` And Maachah the wife of Machir bore a son and she called his name Peresh. And the name of his brother was Sheresh, and his *_sons_* were Ulam and Rakem.
* `1Ch 7:17` And the son of Ulam: Bedan. These were the *_sons_* of Gilead, the son of Machir, the son of Manasseh.
* `1Ch 7:19` And the *_sons_* of Shemida were Ahian, Shechem, Likhi, and Aniam.
* `1Ch 7:20` And the *_sons_* of Ephraim: Shuthelah, Bered his son, Tahath his son, Eladah his son, Tahath his son,
* `1Ch 7:30` The *_sons_* of Asher: Imnah, Isuah, Ishua, and Beriah. Serah was their sister.
* `1Ch 7:31` And the *_sons_* of Beriah: Heber and Malchiel, who is the father of Birzavith.
* `1Ch 7:33` And the *_sons_* of Japhlet: Pasach, Bimhal, and Ashvath. These are the children of Japhlet.
* `1Ch 7:34` And the *_sons_* of Shamer: Ahi, Rohgah, Jehubbah, and Aram.
* `1Ch 7:35` And the *_sons_* of his brother Helem: Zophah, Imna, Shelesh, and Amal.
* `1Ch 7:36` The *_sons_* of Zophah: Suah, Harnepher, Shual, Beri, Imrah,
* `1Ch 7:38` And the *_sons_* of Jether: Jephunneh, Pispah, and Ara.
* `1Ch 7:39` And the *_sons_* of Ulla: Arah, Haniel, and Rezia.
* `1Ch 8:3` And the *_sons_* of Bela were Addar, Gera, Abihud,
* `1Ch 8:6` And these are the *_sons_* of Ehud, who are the heads of the fathers of the inhabitants of Geba, and they carried them captive to Manahath:
* `1Ch 8:10` Jeuz, Shachia, and Mirma. These were his *_sons_*, heads of the fathers.
* `1Ch 8:12` The *_sons_* of Elpaal: Eber, Misham, Shamed (who built Ono and Lod, with its town),
* `1Ch 8:16` Michael, Ispah, and Joha were the *_sons_* of Beriah.
* `1Ch 8:18` Ishmerai, Jezliah, and Jobab were the *_sons_* of Elpaal.
* `1Ch 8:21` Adaiah, Beraiah, and Shimrath were the *_sons_* of Shimhi.
* `1Ch 8:25` Iphedeiah, and Penuel were the *_sons_* of Shashak.
* `1Ch 8:27` Jaresiah, Eliah, and Zichri were the *_sons_* of Jeroham.
* `1Ch 8:35` And the *_sons_* of Micah were Pithon, Melech, Tarea, and Ahaz.
* `1Ch 8:38` And Azel had six *_sons_* whose names are these: Azrikam, Bocheru, Ishmael, Sheariah, Obadiah, and Hanan. All these were the *_sons_* of Azel.
* `1Ch 8:39` And the *_sons_* of Eshek his brother were Ulam his first-born, Jehush the second, and Eliphelet the third.
* `1Ch 8:40` And the *_sons_* of Ulam were mighty men of valor, archers, and had many *_sons_* and *_sons_*' *_sons_* numbering one hundred and fifty. All these are among the *_sons_* of Benjamin.
* `1Ch 9:5` And of the Shilonites: Asaiah the first-born and his *_sons_*.
* `1Ch 9:6` And of the *_sons_* of Zerah: Jeuel and their brothers, six hundred and ninety.
* `1Ch 9:7` And of the *_sons_* of Benjamin: Sallu the son of Meshullam, the son of Hodaviah, the son of Hasenuah,
* `1Ch 9:14` And of the Levites: Shemaiah the son of Hasshub, the son of Azrikam, the son of Hashabiah, of the *_sons_* of Merari;
* `1Ch 9:30` And some of the *_sons_* of the priests made the ointment of the spices.
* `1Ch 9:32` And others of their brothers, of the *_sons_* of the Kohathites, were over the show-bread to prepare it every sabbath.
* `1Ch 9:41` And the *_sons_* of Micah were Pithon, Melech, Tahrea, and Ahaz.
* `1Ch 9:44` And Azel had six *_sons_* whose names are these: Azrikam, Bocheru, Ishmael, Sheariah, Obadiah, and Hanan. These were the *_sons_* of Azel.
* `1Ch 10:2` And the Philistines followed hard after Saul and after his *_sons_*. And the Philistines slew Jonathan, Abinadab, and Malchi-shua, the *_sons_* of Saul.
* `1Ch 10:6` So Saul died, and his three *_sons_* and all his house died together.
* `1Ch 10:7` And when all the men of Israel who were in the valley saw that they fled and that Saul and his *_sons_* were dead, they forsook their cities and fled. And the Philistines came and dwelt in them.
* `1Ch 10:8` And it came to pass on the next day when the Philistines came to strip the slain, that they found Saul and his *_sons_* fallen on mount Gilboa.
* `1Ch 10:12` They arose—all the valiant men—and took away the body of Saul and the bodies of his *_sons_*, brought them to Jabesh, buried their bones under the oak in Jabesh, and fasted seven days.
* `1Ch 11:34` The *_sons_* of Hashem the Gizonite, Jonathan the son of Shage the Hararite,
* `1Ch 11:44` Uzzia the Ashterathite, Shama and Jehiel the *_sons_* of Hothan the Aroerite,
* `1Ch 11:46` Eliel the Mehavite, Jeribai and Joshaviah the *_sons_* of Elnaam, Ithmah the Moabite,
* `1Ch 12:3` The chief was Ahiezer, then Joash (the *_sons_* of Shemaah the Gibeathite), and Jeziel and Pelet (the *_sons_* of Azmaveth), and Berachah and Jehu the Antothite,
* `1Ch 12:7` And Joelah and Zebadiah (the *_sons_* of Jeroham of Gedor).
* `1Ch 12:14` These were of the *_sons_* of Gad, captains of the host. One of the least was over one hundred, and the greatest over one thousand.
* `1Ch 14:3` And David took more wives at Jerusalem. And David begot more *_sons_* and daughters.
* `1Ch 15:5` Of the *_sons_* of Kohath: Uriel the chief and his brothers one hundred and twenty.
* `1Ch 15:6` Of the *_sons_* of Merari: Asaiah the chief and his brothers two hundred and twenty.
* `1Ch 15:7` Of the *_sons_* of Gershom: Joel the chief and his brothers one hundred and thirty.
* `1Ch 15:8` Of the *_sons_* of Elizaphan: Shemaiah the chief and his brothers two hundred.
* `1Ch 15:9` Of the *_sons_* of Hebron: Eliel the chief and his brothers eighty.
* `1Ch 15:10` Of the *_sons_* of Uzziel: Amminadab the chief and his brothers one hundred and twelve.
* `1Ch 15:17` So the Levites appointed Heman the son of Joel; and of his brothers, Asaph the son of Berechiah; and of the *_sons_* of Merari their brothers, Ethan the son of Kushaiah;
* `1Ch 16:42` And with them were Heman and Jeduthun with trumpets and cymbals for those who should make a sound, and with musical instruments of God. And the *_sons_* of Jeduthun were gatekeepers.
* `1Ch 17:11` And it shall come to pass, when your days are fulfilled that you must go to be with your fathers, that I will raise up your offspring after you, who shall be of your *_sons_*, and I will establish his kingdom.
* `1Ch 18:17` Benaiah the son of Jehoiada was over the Cherethites and the Pelethites, and the *_sons_* of David were chief deputies of the king.
* `1Ch 21:20` And Ornan turned back and saw the angel, and his four *_sons_* with him hid themselves. Now Ornan was threshing wheat.
* `1Ch 23:6` And David divided them into courses among the *_sons_* of Levi, namely, Gershon, Kohath, and Merari.
* `1Ch 23:8` The *_sons_* of Laadan: the chief was Jehiel, Zetham, and Joel, three.
* `1Ch 23:9` The *_sons_* of Shimei: Shelomith, Haziel, and Haran, three. These were the chief of the fathers of Laadan.
* `1Ch 23:10` And the *_sons_* of Shimei were Jahath, Zina, Jeush and Beriah. These four were the *_sons_* of Shimei.
* `1Ch 23:11` And Jahath was the chief and Zizah the second, but Jeush and Beriah did not have many *_sons_*; therefore, they were in one reckoning according to their father's house.
* `1Ch 23:12` The *_sons_* of Kohath: Amram, Izhar, Hebron, and Uzziel, four.
* `1Ch 23:13` The *_sons_* of Amram: Aaron and Moses. And Aaron was separated so that he should sanctify the most holy things—he and his *_sons_* forever—to burn incense before the LORD, to minister to him, and to bless in his name forever.
* `1Ch 23:14` Now concerning Moses the man of God, his *_sons_* were named among the tribe of Levi.
* `1Ch 23:15` The *_sons_* of Moses were Gershom and Eliezer.
* `1Ch 23:16` Of the *_sons_* of Gershom: Shebuel was the chief.
* `1Ch 23:17` And the son of Eliezer was Rehabiah the chief. And Eliezer had no other *_sons_*, but the *_sons_* of Rehabiah were very many.
* `1Ch 23:19` Of the *_sons_* of Hebron: Jeriah the first, Amariah the second, Jehaziel the third, and Jekameam the fourth.
* `1Ch 23:20` Of the *_sons_* of Uzziel: Micah the first and Jesiah the second.
* `1Ch 23:21` The *_sons_* of Merari: Mahli and Mushi. The *_sons_* of Mahli: Eleazar and Kish.
* `1Ch 23:22` And Eleazar died and had no *_sons_*, but daughters, and their brothers the *_sons_* of Kish took them.
* `1Ch 23:23` The *_sons_* of Mushi: Mahli, Eder, and Jeremoth, three.
* `1Ch 23:24` These were the *_sons_* of Levi after the house of their fathers, even the chief of the fathers, as they were counted by number of names by their polls, who did the work for the service of the house of the LORD, from the age of twenty years and upward.
* `1Ch 23:28` Because their office was to wait on the *_sons_* of Aaron for the service of the house of the LORD, in the courts, in the chambers, in the purifying of all holy things, and the work of the service of the house of God;
* `1Ch 23:32` And that they should keep the charge of the Tabernacle of the Congregation, the charge of the holy place, and the charge of the *_sons_* of Aaron their brothers in the service of the house of the LORD.
* `1Ch 24:1` Now these are the divisions of the *_sons_* of Aaron: The *_sons_* of Aaron: Nadab, Abihu, Eleazar, and Ithamar.
* `1Ch 24:3` And David distributed them, both Zadok of the *_sons_* of Eleazar and Ahimelech of the *_sons_* of Ithamar, according to their offices in their service.
* `1Ch 24:4` And there were more chief men found among the *_sons_* of Eleazar than among the *_sons_* of Ithamar, and thus they were divided. Among the *_sons_* of Eleazar there were sixteen chief men of the house of their fathers and eight among the *_sons_* of Ithamar according to the house of their fathers.
* `1Ch 24:5` Thus they were divided by lot, one sort with another, for the governors of the sanctuary and governors of the house of God were of the *_sons_* of Eleazar and of the *_sons_* of Ithamar.
* `1Ch 24:20` And the rest of the *_sons_* of Levi were these: Of the *_sons_* of Amram: Shubael. Of the *_sons_* of Shubael: Jehdeiah.
* `1Ch 24:21` Concerning Rehabiah: of the *_sons_* of Rehabiah, the first was Isshiah.
* `1Ch 24:22` Of the Izharites: Shelomoth. Of the *_sons_* of Shelomoth: Jahath.
* `1Ch 24:23` And the *_sons_* of Hebron: Jeriah the first, Amariah the second, Jehaziel the third, and Jekameam the fourth.
* `1Ch 24:24` Of the *_sons_* of Uzziel: Michah. Of the *_sons_* of Michah: Shamir.
* `1Ch 24:25` The brother of Michah was Isshiah. Of the *_sons_* of Isshiah: Zechariah.
* `1Ch 24:26` The *_sons_* of Merari were Mahli and Mushi. The son of Jaaziah: Beno.
* `1Ch 24:27` The *_sons_* of Merari by Jaaziah: Beno, Shoham, Zaccur, and Ibri.
* `1Ch 24:28` Of Mahli came Eleazar, who had no *_sons_*.
* `1Ch 24:30` The *_sons_* also of Mushi: Mahli, Eder, and Jerimoth. These were the *_sons_* of the Levites after the house of their fathers.
* `1Ch 24:31` These likewise cast lots over against their brothers the *_sons_* of Aaron in the presence of David the king, Zadok, Ahimelech, and the chief of the fathers of the priests and Levites, even the principal fathers over against their younger brothers.
* `1Ch 25:1` Moreover, David and the captains of the host separated for the service some of the *_sons_* of Asaph, of Heman, and of Jeduthun, who should prophesy with harps, with psalteries, and with cymbals. And this is the number of the workmen according to their service:
* `1Ch 25:2` Of the *_sons_* of Asaph: Zaccur, Joseph, Nethaniah, and Asarelah, the *_sons_* of Asaph under the hands of Asaph who prophesied according to the order of the king.
* `1Ch 25:3` Of Jeduthun, the *_sons_* of Jeduthun: Gedaliah, Zeri, Jeshaiah, Hashabiah, and Mattithiah, six, under the hands of their father Jeduthun who prophesied with a harp to give thanks and to praise the LORD.
* `1Ch 25:4` Of Heman, the *_sons_* of Heman: Bukkiah, Mattaniah, Uzziel, Shebuel, Jerimoth, Hananiah, Hanani, Eliathah, Giddalti, Romamti-ezer, Joshbekashah, Mallothi, Hothir, and Mahazioth.
* `1Ch 25:5` All these were the *_sons_* of Heman the king's seer in the words of God to lift up the horn. And God gave to Heman fourteen *_sons_* and three daughters.
* `1Ch 25:9` Now the first lot came forth for Asaph to Joseph; the second to Gedaliah—he, his brothers, and *_sons_* were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:10` The third to Zaccur—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:11` The fourth to Izri—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:12` The fifth to Nethaniah—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:13` The sixth to Bukkiah—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:14` The seventh to Jesharelah—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:15` The eighth to Jeshaiah—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:16` The ninth to Mattaniah—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:17` The tenth to Shimei—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:18` The eleventh to Azareel—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:19` The twelfth to Hashabiah—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:20` The thirteenth to Shubael—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:21` The fourteenth to Mattithiah—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:22` The fifteenth to Jeremoth—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:23` The sixteenth to Hananiah—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:24` The seventeenth to Joshbekashah—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:25` The eighteenth to Hanani—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
* `1Ch 25:26` The nineteenth to Mallothi—he, his *_sons_*, and his brothers were twelve;
